问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，U1800（型号为 TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1）的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络详情如下： ### 1. U1800 的输入接口是什么？ * **输入接口**：U1800的电源输入引脚为 **VIN**，连接到 **VCC3V3** 网络（3.3V 电源）。 * **来源**：电路设计引脚映射（证据[1]）。 ### 2. U1800 的输出检测功能是什么？ * **输出检测功能**：U1800通过 **VOSNS** 和 **GOSNS** 引脚实现差分输出电压检测。 * **VOSNS**（输出电压检测引脚）连接到 **VCC0V75** 网络。 * **GOSNS**（输出地检测引脚）连接到 **GND**。 * **来源**：电路设计引脚映射（证据[1]）。 ### 3. U1800 的使能功能是什么？ * **使能功能**：U1800的使能引脚为 **EN**，连接到 **N19888813** 网络。根据数据手册，该引脚用于控制芯片的开启与关闭（高电平使能，低电平禁用）。 * **来源**：电路设计引脚映射（证据[1]）及数据手册（证据[8]，描述EN引脚为“enable pin”）。 ### 4. U1800 的 I²C 控制网络是什么？ * **I²C 控制网络**：U1800的I²C控制接口由以下引脚组成： * **SDA**（串行数据线）：连接到 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** 网络。 * **SCL**（串行时钟线）：连接到 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** 网络。 * **来源**：电路设计引脚映射（证据[1]）。 ### 缺失信息与冲突说明 * **缺失信息**：所有子问题均已覆盖，无缺失项。 * **证据冲突**：在提供的证据覆盖度中，存在字段冲突（如 `fsw`, `ob`, `load`, `swen` 等），但这些冲突值均涉及芯片的 **内部配置或电气特性参数**（如开关频率、负载阻值、过流模式等），与用户问题要求的 **“输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络”** 这些特定引脚功能无关。因此，这些冲突不影响当前问题的回答。

The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 | window comparator monitors the output voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | If the junction temperature falls below the thermal shutdown threshold of 150°C (typical), the device: · Starts switching again, starting with a new soft-start sequence · Sets the EN pin to high impedance ·Sets the PG pin to high-impedance The device clears the TSHUT bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TSHUT threshold of 150°C (typical). In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | <table><caption> over operating junction temperature (Tj = -40 °℃ to 150 °C) and Vin = 2.7 V to 6 V. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. All functions are available in on mode. The device leaves on mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves on mode and enters standby mode when any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. · The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. 9.5 Programming 9.5.1 Serial Interface Description I2C is a 2-wire serial interface developed by Philips Semiconductor, now NXP Semiconductors (see I2C-Bus Specification and User Manual, Revision 6, 4 April 2014). The bus consists of a data line (SDA) and a clock line (SCL) with pullup structures. When the bus is idle, both SDA and SCL lines are pulled high. All I2C-compatible devices connect to the I2C bus through open-drain I/O pins, SDA and SCL. A controller, usually a microcontroller or a digital signal processor, controls the bus. The controller is responsible for generating the SCL signal and device addresses. The controller also generates specific conditions that indicate the START and STOP of data transfer. A target receives data, transmits data, or both on the bus under control of the controller. The TPS6287x-Q1 device operates as a target and supports the following data transfer modes, as defined in the I2C-Bus Specification: standard mode (100 kbps), fast mode (400 kbps), and fast mode plus (1 Mbps). The interface adds flexibility to the power supply solution, enabling most functions to be programmed to new values depending on the instantaneous application requirements. Register contents remain intact as long as the input voltage remains above 1.4 V. The data transfer protocol for standard and fast modes is exactly the same, therefore, they are referred to as F/S-mode in this document. The device supports 7-bit addressing; general call addresses are not supported. | <table><caption> Table 9-16. In POR mode, no functions are available and the content of the device registers is not valid. The device leaves POR mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage increases above the POR threshold 9.4.2 Undervoltage Lockout The device operates in UVLO mode when the supply voltage is between the POR and UVLO thresholds. If the device enters UVLO mode from POR mode, no functions are available. If the device enters UVLO mode from standby mode, the output discharge function is available. The content of the device registers is valid in UVLO mode. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters POR mode when the supply voltage decreases below the POR threshold. The device leaves UVLO mode and enters standby mode when the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold. 9.4.3 Standby The device operates in standby mode when the supply voltage is greater than the UVLO threshold (and the device has completed its initialization) and any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register.· The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. The device initializes for 4o0 μs (typical) after the supply voltage increases above the UVLO threshold voltage following a device power-on reset. If the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold but not below the POR threshold, the device does not reinitialize when the supply voltage increases again. During initialization, the device reads the state of the FSEL, VSEL, and SYNC_OUT pins. The following functions are available in standby mode: · I2C interface ·Output discharge ·Power good Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The device leaves standby mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves standby mode and enters on mode when all of the following conditions are true: · A high-level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | all operating conditions. 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：## 答案 ### 子问题 1：从 VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源路径中，经过的负载开关是哪一颗？ **结论**：存在一个负载开关将 VCC1V8 转换为 VCC1V8_EQ 用于 EQ 供电，但该负载开关的**具体器件型号**在现有证据中未明确给出。 **支撑证据**： - 文档 [18] 明确说明：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, now using the load switch scheme is selected.” - 原理图 [13] 显示了网络 VCC1V8_EQ（连接 C1830、C664、C667、L600‑L604、R1141、TP1817、U1803.QOD、U1803.VOUT 等），但未直接标识负载开关的器件编号。U1803 与其他电源相关，但未标明为负载开关。 **缺失信息**：负载开关的具体型号（如 Ux 编号）。 --- ### 子问题 2：该负载开关的使能信号是什么？ **状态**：根据检索账本，该子问题被标记为 **missing**（未覆盖），但我在检索到的文档 [18] 中找到了明确信息，请谨慎参考。 **可参考证据**： 文档 [18] 中写道：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, ... 用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 即使能信号为 **MCU_P20.0**（或称为 EQ6L_GPIO_A0_P23.0？注意原理图中 P23.0 与 P20.0 不同，此处以文档为准）。 **缺口说明**： - 检索账本中 sq_2 的 supporting_evidence_ids 为空，未将任何电路或表格证据直接关联到该使能信号。 - 文档 [18] 属于文本描述，并非原理图连接或管脚定义，其可靠性低于直接电路证据。 - 原理图 [3] 中网络 EQ6L_GPIO_A0_P23.0 连接至 R541、TP1024、U900E.P23.0，但未显示与负载开关的直接连接，因此无法确认该 GPIO 实际用于使能该负载开关。 **建议**：需要进一步查阅原理图或表格中关于负载开关使能引脚的连接，以确认 MCU_P20.0 是否确实连接到该负载开关的 EN 引脚。 --- ### 缺失信息汇总 | 缺失项 | 说明 | |--------|------| | 负载开关的具体器件型号 | 仅知存在负载开关，但未识别出 Ux 编号 | | 使能信号的直接连接证据 | 文档提供了使能引脚（MCU_P20.0），但缺乏原理图或表格中的直接连接确认 | ### 说明 - 以上分析严格基于给定证据，未编造任何信息。 - 证据冲突列表中的字段（如 mcu_0v75_mipi_ddr_en、mcu_vcc0v75_en、ecu_en）与本题无关，故未列出。 - 如需进一步确认，建议使用电路查询工具，以“负载开关+使能信号”为关键词，在原理图文件 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF 中搜索相关连接。
评分正文：## 答案 ### 子问题 1：从 VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源路径中，经过的负载开关是哪一颗？ **结论**：存在一个负载开关将 VCC1V8 转换为 VCC1V8_EQ 用于 EQ 供电，但该负载开关的**具体器件型号**在现有证据中未明确给出。 **支撑证据**： - 文档 [18] 明确说明：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, now using the load switch scheme is selected.” - 原理图 [13] 显示了网络 VCC1V8_EQ（连接 C1830、C664、C667、L600‑L604、R1141、TP1817、U1803.QOD、U1803.VOUT 等），但未直接标识负载开关的器件编号。U1803 与其他电源相关，但未标明为负载开关。 **缺失信息**：负载开关的具体型号（如 Ux 编号）。 --- ### 子问题 2：该负载开关的使能信号是什么？ **状态**：根据检索账本，该子问题被标记为 **missing**（未覆盖），但我在检索到的文档 [18] 中找到了明确信息，请谨慎参考。 **可参考证据**： 文档 [18] 中写道：“VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, ... 用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 即使能信号为 **MCU_P20.0**（或称为 EQ6L_GPIO_A0_P23.0？注意原理图中 P23.0 与 P20.0 不同，此处以文档为准）。 **缺口说明**： - 检索账本中 sq_2 的 supporting_evidence_ids 为空，未将任何电路或表格证据直接关联到该使能信号。 - 文档 [18] 属于文本描述，并非原理图连接或管脚定义，其可靠性低于直接电路证据。 - 原理图 [3] 中网络 EQ6L_GPIO_A0_P23.0 连接至 R541、TP1024、U900E.P23.0，但未显示与负载开关的直接连接，因此无法确认该 GPIO 实际用于使能该负载开关。 **建议**：需要进一步查阅原理图或表格中关于负载开关使能引脚的连接，以确认 MCU_P20.0 是否确实连接到该负载开关的 EN 引脚。 --- ### 缺失信息汇总 | 缺失项 | 说明 | |--------|------| | 负载开关的具体器件型号 | 仅知存在负载开关，但未识别出 Ux 编号 | | 使能信号的直接连接证据 | 文档提供了使能引脚（MCU_P20.0），但缺乏原理图或表格中的直接连接确认 | ### 说明 - 以上分析严格基于给定证据，未编造任何信息。 - 证据冲突列表中的字段（如 mcu_0v75_mipi_ddr_en、mcu_vcc0v75_en、ecu_en）与本题无关，故未列出。 - 如需进一步确认，建议使用电路查询工具，以“负载开关+使能信号”为关键词，在原理图文件 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF 中搜索相关连接。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F15</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>上电模式、下电模式、运行模式 Power on mode, Power off mode, Run mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>1、 上电模式 使能信号MCU_0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN=H，二级电源SA23002开始工作，为负载提供0V75_MIPI_DDR的电源 2、 下电模式 使能信号MCU_0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L后，二级电源停止0V75_MIPI_DDR供电。 3、 运行模式 二级电源SA23002正常输出0V75_MIPI_DDR，系统正常工作。 Power on mode The enable signal MCU_0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN=H, the secondary power supply SA23002 starts to work and provides power to the 0V75_MIPI_DDR for the load. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：好的，这是根据您提供的证据和检索结果，对您的问题进行的回答。 ### 1. 文档中声明了哪些CAN相关模块？ 根据提供的文档证据，板上声明了以下四个CAN相关模块： * **CAN0 (F23)**：基于 TCAN1044A 的CAN通讯。[16] * **CAN1 (F22)**：基于 TCAN1044A 的CAN通讯。[16] * **CAN2 (F21)**：基于 TCAN1044A 的CAN通讯。[16] * **CAN3 (F20)**：基于 TCAN1145 的CAN通讯，硬件支持特定报文唤醒。[16] **来源：** * [16] 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的“模块功能列表”明确列出了F20、F21、F22、F23对应的CAN模块及其主要芯片型号。 * [17] 同一文档的另一部分也确认了“ADAS系统需求MCU外置4路CAN/CAN FD和外部进行通讯”，并描述了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3的引脚分配。[17] ### 2. 能否用网表确认其具体连接？ **可以部分确认。** 网表提供了这些CAN模块在电路中的部分物理连接信息，但无法完全确认其所有连接，特别是与MCU的连接。 **基于网表（电路图 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）确认的连接如下：** #### CAN0 模块的连接 * **CAN总线连接**：CAN0H 和 CAN0L 网络连接到 `X1900` 连接器，这是对外通信的接口。 * `CAN0H` 连接：`C1201`, `D1200`, `L1200`, `R1209`, `R1214`, `TP1206`, `X1900`。[1] * `CAN0L` 连接：`C1200`, `D1200`, `L1200`, `R1212`, `R1215`, `TP1215`, `X1900`。[2] * **INH（控制/唤醒）信号**：`CAN0_INH` 网络连接到 `U1200.INH` 和 `U1203.INH`，这两个很可能是CAN收发器。同时，`CAN0_INH_ACQ` 网络将此信号连接到MCU的ADC采样引脚 `U900A.AN20`。[3][4] * **STB（待机控制）信号**：`CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8` 网络将该信号连接到 `U1200.SCSN`, `U1202.STB`, `U1203.NSTB`，表明这是由MCU的SPI片选信号控制的。[5] #### CAN1 模块的连接 * **CAN总线连接**：CAN1H 和 CAN1L 网络连接到 `X1900` 连接器。 * `CAN1H` 连接：`C1207`, `D1201`, `L1201`, `R1220`, `R1224`, `TP1222`, `X1900`。[6] * `CAN1L` 连接：`C1206`, `D1201`, `L1201`, `R1223`, `R1225`, `TP1223`, `X1900`。[7] * **INH信号**：`CAN1_INH` 网络连接到 `U1204.INH` 和 `U1205.INH`。[8] * **STB信号**：**未在网表中找到** `CAN1_STB` 相关的网络。网表中显示了 `CAN0_STB` 和 `CAN2_STB`，但未找到对应CAN1的STB网络。 #### CAN2 模块的连接 * **CAN总线连接**：CAN2H 和 CAN2L 网络连接到 `X1900` 连接器。 * `CAN2H` 连接：`C1301`, `D1300`, `L1300`, `R1302`, `R1306`, `TP1301`, `X1900`。[9] * `CAN2L` 连接：`C1300`, `D1300`, `L1300`, `R1305`, `R1307`, `TP1303`, `X1900`。[10] * **INH信号**：`CAN2_INH` 网络连接到 `U1302.INH` 和 `U1303.INH`。[11] `CAN2_INH_ACQ` 网络将此信号连接到MCU的ADC采样引脚 `U900A.AN22`。[12] * **STB信号**：`CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5` 网络将该信号连接到 `U1300.STB`, `U1302.SCSN`, `U1303.NSTB` 以及MCU的引脚 `U900D.P00.5`。[13] #### CAN3 模块的连接 * **网表中未找到直接对应CAN3的网络。** 证据中未列出以 `CAN3` 开头的网络（如 `CAN3H`, `CAN3L`, `CAN3_INH` 等）。 **来源：** * 所有连接信息均来自电路图文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中的网表记录 [1] 至 [13]。 ### 3. 缺失信息 * **CAN1模块的STB连接**：网表中未包含CAN1的待机控制（STB）网络，无法确认其具体连接。 * **CAN3模块的物理连接**：网表中未包含任何以CAN3命名的网络，无法通过提供的网表确认其总线和控制信号的具体连接。 * **MCU与CAN模块的连接**：网表仅显示了部分信号（如INH_ACQ和STB）连接到MCU。CAN模块的TX、RX等数据引脚与MCU的连接在网表中未体现，无法确认。 ### 4. 证据冲突 以下是证据中存在的冲突，**不能合并为确定结论**： * **CAN0、CAN1、CAN2的收发器型号**： * 证据[16] 声明 CAN0、CAN1、CAN2 均基于 `TCAN1044A`。 * 证据[15] 在描述运行模式时，同时提到了 `TCAN1044A` 和 `TCAN1145`，但未明确其对应哪个CAN模块。 * 证据[17] 声明“四路CAN/CANFD都需要兼容TCAN1145和TCAN1044A，根据客户需求进行选贴”，这意味着CAN0、CAN1、CAN2的型号并非固定为TCAN1044A，而是与CAN3一样可能为TCAN1145。 * **CAN3 (TCAN1145) 的寄存器配置值 (MC)**： * 证据[15] 在描述TCAN1145的不同模式时，对 `MC` 寄存器给出了不同的值： * Normal模式：`MC=111` * Standby模式：`MC=100` * Sleep模式：`MC=001` * 这些值描述了不同的工作模式，不是冲突，而是不同模式下的配置要求。此处无冲突，仅为模式说明。 **总结：** 文档清晰地声明了板上有四个CAN模块。网表可以确认CAN0、CAN1、CAN2的CAN总线物理连接和部分控制信号，但无法给出CAN3和CAN1的STB信号连接。关于收发器型号，存在“固定为TCAN1044A”与“兼容TCAN1145和TCAN1044A，按需选贴”的冲突。

Normal模式：`MC=111` * Standby模式：`MC=100` * Sleep模式：`MC=001` * 这些值描述了不同的工作模式，不是冲突，而是不同模式下的配置要求。此处无冲突，仅为模式说明。 **总结：** 文档清晰地声明了板上有四个CAN模块。网表可以确认CAN0、CAN1、CAN2的CAN总线物理连接和部分控制信号，但无法给出CAN3和CAN1的STB信号连接。关于收发器型号，存在“固定为TCAN1044A”与“兼容TCAN1145和TCAN1044A，按需选贴”的冲突。

After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | 顾客代表Customer Representative其他与项目相关人员等Other relevant person术语及缩写解释 Terms and AbbreviationsTable 1-1Terms and Abbreviations参考文档 Reference DocumentTable 1-2Reference Document硬件架构 Hardware Architecture硬件架构描述了60060894_ADAS项目的硬件基础模块，模块和模块之间的接口。The Hardware Architecture describe the HW basic component of 600608964_ADAS project, as well as the interface of each component.系统概述 System overviewADAS产品要求功能安全达到ASIL-B，硬件整体架构介绍如下：ADAS products require functional safety to meet ASIL-B. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | Updated theDevice OptionsTable. 3 .5 Modified MODE/SYNC pin Description in Pin Functions Changed the title of Figure 8-5 12 Added approximate minimum on time value. 15 Added behavior of device when EN is toggled and input voltage is applied.. .17 · Modified Table 9-5 to reflect new available Orderable Part Number.. 21 Added a comment on how to connect secondary devices SYNC_OUT pin.. 27 Added a footnote to Table 9-10 .31 Added I2C Register Reset .34 Added Number of phases NΦ and adapted passive component selection equations in Typical Application ...41 Page Changes from Revision * (May 2022) to Revision A (July 2022) Changed document status from Advance Information to Production Data...... .1 5 Description (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. For more information, see Table 9-5 and (2) 7 Pin Configuration and Functions 8 Specifications 8.1 Absolute Maximum Ratings over operating temperature range (unless otherwise noted)(1) (1)Operation outside the Absolute Maximum Ratings may cause permanent device damage. (2)All voltage values are with respect to the GND pin. While switching. (3) | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | A stacked configuration uses a daisy-chained clocking signal, in which each device switches with a phase offset of approximately 140° relative to the adjacent devices in the daisy-chain. To daisy-chain the clocking signal, connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the primary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the first secondary device. Connect the SYNC_OUT pin of the first secondary device to the MODE/SYNC pin of the second secondary device. Continue this connection scheme for all devices in the stack, to daisy-chain them together. Hiccup overcurrent protection must not be used in a stacked configuration. In a stacked configuration, the common enable signal also acts as a SYSTEM_READY signal (see Section 9.3.3). Each device in the stack can pull its EN pin low during device start-up or when a fault occurs. Thus, the stack is only enabled when all devices have completed their start-up sequence and are fault-free. A fault in any one device disables the whole stack for as long as the fault condition exists. During start-up, the primary converter pulls the COMP pin low for as long as the enable signal (SYSTEM_READY) is low. When the enable signal goes high, the primary device actively controls the COMP pin and all converters in the stack follow the COMP voltage. During start-up, each device in the stack pulls its PG pin low while it initializes. When initialization is complete, each secondary device in the stack sets its PG pin to a high impedance and the primary device alone controls the state of the PG signal. The PG pin goes high when the stack has completed its start-up ramp and the output voltage is within specification. The secondary converters in the stack detect the rising edge of the power-good signal and switch from DCM operation to CCM operation. After the stack has successfully started up, the primary device controls the power-good signal in the normal way. In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices, and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. But a thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Functionality During Stacked Operation Some device features are not available during stacked operation, or are only available in the primary converter. Fault Handling During Stacked Operation In a stacked configuration, there are some faults that only affect individual devices and other faults that affect all devices. For example, if one device enters current limit, only that device is affected. A thermal shutdown or undervoltage lockout event in one device disables all devices through the shared enable (SYSTEM_READY) signal. Table 9-9 summarizes the fault handling of the TPS6287x-Q1 devices during stacked operation. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V.

<table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | MCU Component ：F01/ MCU; None：QM; None：E11; None：P12.1; None：P12.1/GTM_TIM4_IN1_6/GTM_TIM3_IN1_6/GETH_MDIOC/GTM_TOUT129/CAN00_TXD/GETH_MDIO; None：GPIO; None：MCU_nINT_PMIC_P12.1; None：/; None：I; None：LP87702的输出中断信号。 高：无中断信号 低：输出中断信号; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 HW Interface Component ：QM(B); None：F13/POWER F14/POWER; None：MCU配置LP87702、TPS62872; MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：E14; None：P15.5; None：P15.5/GTM_TIM3_IN0_4/GTM_TIM2_IN0_4/QSPI2_MTSRA/GTM_TOUT76/QSPI2_MTSR/MSC0_EN0/I2C0_SDA; None：I2C0_SDAC; None：MCU_SDA_P15.5; None：/; None：IO; None：IIC_SDA信号 外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08 TPS62872地址：0x41; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 MCU Component ：F01/MCU; None：B; None：A17; None：P15.4; None：P15.4/GTM_TIM3_IN7_4/GTM_TIM2_IN7_4/I2C0_SCLC/QSPI2_MRSTA/SCU_E_REQ0_0/GTM_TOUT75/QSPI2_MRST; None：I2C0_SCL; None：MCU_SCL_P15.4; None：/; None：O; None：IIC_CLK信号 外挂LP87702和TPS62872 LP87702地址：0x08 TPS62872地址：0x41; None：HI-Z，硬件外部上拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | <table><tr><td>序号</td><td>模块序号</td><td>模块名称</td><td>功能定义</td><td>功能安全需求</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>F013</td><td>POWER</td><td>LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>1</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample1</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>F14</td><td>POWER</td><td>TPS62872，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC0V75电压输出，EQ6L核电供电</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>2</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>F15</td><td>POWER</td><td>SA23002，VCC3V3输入，0V75_MIPI_DDR电压输出</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>3</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>4</td><td>F16</td><td>POWER</td><td>TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC3V3_EQ输出</td><td>QM(B)</td></tr><tr><td>4</td><td>F01</td><td>MCU</td><td>SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA</td><td>B(B)</td></tr><tr><td>4</td><td>F08</td><td>EQ system power sample</td><td>EQ6L系统供电电源电压ADC采样滤波、分压电路</td><td>B(B)</td></tr></table> | Figure 9-14. 问题：VCC3V3 在板上主要给哪些电源器件供电？不要把电阻电容当成主要负载。
参考答案：VCC3V3 是主要 3.3V 电源轨，供给 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600 等 IC；回答应聚焦芯片而非无源器件。
实际回答：根据现有证据，VCC3V3在板上主要供电给以下电源器件（排除电阻电容）： ### 子问题1：VCC3V3网络连接了哪些电源器件（排除电阻电容）？ **回答：** 基于电路原理图证据，VCC3V3网络直接连接了以下电源器件的输入引脚（包括电源转换芯片、负载开关、连接器端口等，不包含电阻、电容、电感、测试点等）： 1. **U1500**：位号U1500，连接至其VIN引脚（电源输入）。证据来源[1]：`U1500.VIN`。 2. **U1600**：位号U1600，连接至其VCCIN引脚。证据来源[1]：`U1600.VCCIN`。 3. **U1700**：位号U1700，连接至其多个电源输入引脚：`VIN_B0_1`, `VIN_B1_1`, `VANA`, `VIN_B0_2`, `VIN_B1_2`。证据来源[1]。 4. **U1800**：位号U1800，连接至其VIN引脚（出现两次）和MODE/SYNC引脚。证据来源[1]：`U1800.VIN`, `U1800.MODE/SYNC`。 5. **U1802**：位号U1802，连接至其VIN引脚。证据来源[1]：`U1802.VIN`。 6. **U1804**：位号U1804，连接至其VIN引脚（出现两次）。证据来源[1]：`U1804.VIN`。 7. **X2000**：连接器，位号X2000，连接至其引脚1和3。证据来源[1]：`X2000.&1`, `X2000.&3`。 **功能说明（来自文档证据）：** * **U1700**：文档证据[17]指出，位号F14/HM ID的模块功能为“VCC3V3转VCC0V75”，用于EQ6的内核供电，方案为TI的TPS62872。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC0V75。此模块的电源芯片很可能就是U1700。 * **U1802**：文档证据[19]指出，位号F16/HM ID的模块功能为“VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ”，用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，方案为负载开关TPS22918。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC3V3_EQ。此模块的电源芯片很可能就是U1802。 * **U1800**：文档证据[18]指出，存在一个电源转换芯片SA23002，将VCC3V3转VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR。此模块的电源芯片很可能就是U1800。 * **U1804**：文档证据[20]指出，存在一个电源模块（HM ID: F13），使用LP87702实现VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50三路电源输出。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50。此模块的电源芯片很可能与U1804相关。 * **U1500**：文档证据[5]显示，VCC3V3_ETH网络由U1500的QOD和VOUT引脚输出，且U1500的VIN引脚连接VCC3V3。这表明U1500是一个将VCC3V3转换为VCC3V3_ETH的电源芯片（可能是负载开关或LDO）。 * **U1600**：目前证据未明确说明其具体功能。文档证据[5]中VCC3V3_ETH网络连接了U1501.EN和U1501.IN，但未提及U1600。 * **X2000**：连接器，用于将外部VCC3V3电源引入板内。 **缺失信息：** * 证据中缺少U1600（位号U1600）的具体功能描述和输出网络，无法确认其用途。 * 证据中未明确说明U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804、U1500单个器件的具体位号与模块功能（如TPS62872、TPS22918、SA23002、LP87702）的对应关系。上述对应关系是基于功能和网络连接的推断。 * 证据中未明确说明VCC3V3直接供电给哪些主控芯片（如MCU、EQ6）的IO电源引脚，虽然VCC3V3通过电源芯片转换后间接供电，但直接连接网络主要为电源转换芯片的输入。 **证据冲突：** * 证据中不存在与“VCC3V3主要给哪些电源器件供电”这一子问题直接相关的冲突。`coverage_matrix.conflicts`中列出的冲突涉及其他字段（如`hw interface component`、`none`、`ecu connector`），与当前问题无关。
评分正文：根据现有证据，VCC3V3在板上主要供电给以下电源器件（排除电阻电容）： ### 子问题1：VCC3V3网络连接了哪些电源器件（排除电阻电容）？ **回答：** 基于电路原理图证据，VCC3V3网络直接连接了以下电源器件的输入引脚（包括电源转换芯片、负载开关、连接器端口等，不包含电阻、电容、电感、测试点等）： 1. **U1500**：位号U1500，连接至其VIN引脚（电源输入）。证据来源[1]：`U1500.VIN`。 2. **U1600**：位号U1600，连接至其VCCIN引脚。证据来源[1]：`U1600.VCCIN`。 3. **U1700**：位号U1700，连接至其多个电源输入引脚：`VIN_B0_1`, `VIN_B1_1`, `VANA`, `VIN_B0_2`, `VIN_B1_2`。证据来源[1]。 4. **U1800**：位号U1800，连接至其VIN引脚（出现两次）和MODE/SYNC引脚。证据来源[1]：`U1800.VIN`, `U1800.MODE/SYNC`。 5. **U1802**：位号U1802，连接至其VIN引脚。证据来源[1]：`U1802.VIN`。 6. **U1804**：位号U1804，连接至其VIN引脚（出现两次）。证据来源[1]：`U1804.VIN`。 7. **X2000**：连接器，位号X2000，连接至其引脚1和3。证据来源[1]：`X2000.&1`, `X2000.&3`。 **功能说明（来自文档证据）：** * **U1700**：文档证据[17]指出，位号F14/HM ID的模块功能为“VCC3V3转VCC0V75”，用于EQ6的内核供电，方案为TI的TPS62872。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC0V75。此模块的电源芯片很可能就是U1700。 * **U1802**：文档证据[19]指出，位号F16/HM ID的模块功能为“VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ”，用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，方案为负载开关TPS22918。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC3V3_EQ。此模块的电源芯片很可能就是U1802。 * **U1800**：文档证据[18]指出，存在一个电源转换芯片SA23002，将VCC3V3转VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR。此模块的电源芯片很可能就是U1800。 * **U1804**：文档证据[20]指出，存在一个电源模块（HM ID: F13），使用LP87702实现VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50三路电源输出。该模块的输入为VCC3V3，输出为VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50。此模块的电源芯片很可能与U1804相关。 * **U1500**：文档证据[5]显示，VCC3V3_ETH网络由U1500的QOD和VOUT引脚输出，且U1500的VIN引脚连接VCC3V3。这表明U1500是一个将VCC3V3转换为VCC3V3_ETH的电源芯片（可能是负载开关或LDO）。 * **U1600**：目前证据未明确说明其具体功能。文档证据[5]中VCC3V3_ETH网络连接了U1501.EN和U1501.IN，但未提及U1600。 * **X2000**：连接器，用于将外部VCC3V3电源引入板内。 **缺失信息：** * 证据中缺少U1600（位号U1600）的具体功能描述和输出网络，无法确认其用途。 * 证据中未明确说明U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804、U1500单个器件的具体位号与模块功能（如TPS62872、TPS22918、SA23002、LP87702）的对应关系。上述对应关系是基于功能和网络连接的推断。 * 证据中未明确说明VCC3V3直接供电给哪些主控芯片（如MCU、EQ6）的IO电源引脚，虽然VCC3V3通过电源芯片转换后间接供电，但直接连接网络主要为电源转换芯片的输入。 **证据冲突：** * 证据中不存在与“VCC3V3主要给哪些电源器件供电”这一子问题直接相关的冲突。`coverage_matrix.conflicts`中列出的冲突涉及其他字段（如`hw interface component`、`none`、`ecu connector`），与当前问题无关。

Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . 问题：软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节？
参考答案：应查 HSI 7.2 节“LP87702 配置”，其中给出软件寄存器和 I²C 配置说明。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，软件配置 LP87702 应查阅 HSI 文档的 **第7.2节“LP87702配置”**。 **来源说明：** - 证据[2]（文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）中明确列出了“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”小节，并说明“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示”。 - 证据[3]（同一文档的目录结构）中显示，第7章为“其他芯片配置”，其下第7.2节即为“LP87702配置 LP87702 Configuration”。 **关键描述：** 该章节（7.2节）包含了LP87702在运行期间需要MCU通过I2C进行配置的寄存器信息。 **缺失信息：** 无。证据已直接覆盖问题，未发现缺失项。 **证据冲突说明：** 本次检索的证据覆盖度报告中存在冲突项，但均与LP87702配置章节的查询无关，例如： - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中，表格字段 `none` 对应的多个取值（如`1`、`a17`、`b`等）存在冲突。 - 文档 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中，表格字段 `td colspan` 对应取值（`2`、`3`、`4`）存在冲突，字段 `operation disabled 1b` 对应取值（`hiccup`、`ssc`）存在冲突。 这些冲突不影响本问题“软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节”的结论。
评分正文：根据提供的证据，软件配置 LP87702 应查阅 HSI 文档的 **第7.2节“LP87702配置”**。 **关键描述：** 该章节（7.2节）包含了LP87702在运行期间需要MCU通过I2C进行配置的寄存器信息。 **缺失信息：** 无。证据已直接覆盖问题，未发现缺失项。 **证据冲突说明：** 本次检索的证据覆盖度报告中存在冲突项，但均与LP87702配置章节的查询无关，例如： - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中，表格字段 `none` 对应的多个取值（如`1`、`a17`、`b`等）存在冲突。 - 文档 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中，表格字段 `td colspan` 对应取值（`2`、`3`、`4`）存在冲突，字段 `operation disabled 1b` 对应取值（`hiccup`、`ssc`）存在冲突。 这些冲突不影响本问题“软件配置 LP87702 应查 HSI 的哪一节”的结论。

READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. 问题：网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377，这两个说法怎么处理？
参考答案：必须保留来源差异：EDF 的 U900 型号是 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，而 HSI 章节名是 TC377 最小系统；现有证据不足以把二者无条件视为同一准确型号。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我将对“网表写 TC367、接口文档写 TC377”这一问题进行详细分析。 ### 核心发现 网表和接口文档存在**器件型号不一致**的冲突。网表设计实际使用了 TC367 芯片，而接口文档的描述和部分标题引用了 TC377 芯片。这是一个需要澄清的设计矛盾。 --- ### 详细分析 #### 1. 网表中 TC367 出现在哪个模块或信号？ **结论：** 网表（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）明确使用了 **SAK-TC367DP-64F300S** 系列芯片（型号为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA` 和 `AAB`），并定义了多个以 `TC367_` 命名的专用网络。 **支持证据：** * **实例声明：** 网表中的两个实例 `U900A` 和 `U900B` 均声明为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S` 系列器件（证据[7]和[8]）。这表明设计中的主控芯片是 **TC367**。 * **网络信号：** 网表中存在 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`, `TC367_JTAG_TDI`, `TC367_JTAG_TDO/DAP2`, `TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1`, `TC367_JTAG_TRST`, `TC367_RESET_N` 等以 `TC367` 命名的网络（证据[1]至[6]）。这些网络直接连接到主芯片 `U900A` 和 `U900B`，进一步确认了芯片型号。 #### 2. 接口文档中 TC377 出现在哪个模块或信号？ **结论：** 接口文档（`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）在**模块功能描述**和**目录结构**中明确提及了 **TC377**，但在**具体的引脚信号定义表格**中，却使用了 `TC367_` 开头的网络名称。 **支持证据：** * **模块描述写 TC377：** 文档在“TC377最小系统”模块中描述道：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片...”（证据[9]）。文档目录也包含“TC377最小系统”章节（证据[11]）。 * **引脚信号表用 TC367：** 在文档的“ECU-MCU信号对应关系”部分（证据[18]），引脚信号列表中的“网络名称”列明确写着 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1` 等，与网表中的命名完全一致。这表明接口文档的底层引脚定义是基于 **TC367** 的。 #### 3. 是否有工程变更通知或勘误说明涉及这两个型号？ **结论：** **没有找到**任何工程变更通知（ECN）或勘误说明来直接解释 TC367 和 TC377 之间的差异。 **支持证据：** * 文档的变更历史（证据[19]）中记录了多次模板和内容的修订，但未提及涉及器件型号从 TC367 变更为 TC377 或反之的变更。 * 证据[11]中虽然提到了“TC377最小系统”，但并未给出任何关于为何描述与网表型号不同的解释或注释。 --- ### 关键信息缺失 1. **缺失明确的工程变更记录：** 没有找到任何官方说明（如ECN、勘误表、设计注释）来解释为何网表使用 TC367，而接口文档描述为 TC377。 2. **缺失兼容性说明：** 没有证据说明 TC367 和 TC377 在硬件设计上是否完全兼容（如 pin-to-pin兼容），因此无法判断这是否是一个可以忽略的命名错误，还是需要修改设计的重大差异。 --- ### 证据冲突（必须单独列出） 根据提供的证据，存在以下关键冲突，**不应合并为确定结论**： | 冲突字段 | 值1 | 值2 | 来源 | 冲突原因 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | | **器件型号** | **TC367** (`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`) | **TC377** (`SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`) | 网表（实例 U900A/B） vs 接口文档（模块描述） | 网表实际使用的芯片是 TC367，而接口文档的描述是 TC377。这是最核心的矛盾。 | | 电源电压 | 3.3V | 1.2-1.35V | 接口文档内部（证据[9]） | 同一文档中，“电源需求”部分描述 MCU 需要 VCC3V3_MCU（3.3V±10%）和 VCC1V25_MCU（1.2-1.35V），但未明确哪个值对应哪个型号，这在不同型号的芯片间可能不同。 | | 网络名称 | `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0` 等 | `TC377` (模块标题) | 接口文档内部 | 文档的**引脚信号表**使用 `TC367_` 命名，但**模块描述**使用 `TC377`。这导致文档自身内部不一致。 | --- ### 总结与建议 **问题性质：** 这是一个**设计文档与实现不一致**的典型问题，属于**严重冲突**。 **处理建议：** 1. **首要行动：** 必须向硬件设计团队和系统架构师**澄清正确的器件型号**。是网表错误（实际应使用 TC377，但用了 TC367 的物料和网络名），还是接口文档错误（描述为 TC377，但实际设计是 TC367）？ 2. **根本原因分析：** 如果它们是 pin-to-pin 兼容的替代型号，需要确认是否是物料替换导致的文档更新滞后。如果不是，则必须修改其中一方的设计以保持一致性。 3. **更新文档：** 根据确认的结果，统一更新网表或接口文档，确保所有描述（实例、网络名、模块描述、引脚信号表）都使用**同一个、正确的**器件型号。 **结论：** 您不能直接忽略这个差异。网表和接口文档在关键的器件型号上存在直接冲突，且缺乏任何解释该差异的变更记录。在获得明确的型号确认和变更说明之前，这份设计存在风险。

`TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TMS/DAP1` 等，与网表中的命名完全一致。这表明接口文档的底层引脚定义是基于 **TC367** 的。 #### 3. 是否有工程变更通知或勘误说明涉及这两个型号？ **结论：** **没有找到**任何工程变更通知（ECN）或勘误说明来直接解释 TC367 和 TC377 之间的差异。 **支持证据：** * 文档的变更历史（证据[19]）中记录了多次模板和内容的修订，但未提及涉及器件型号从 TC367 变更为 TC377 或反之的变更。 * 证据[11]中虽然提到了“TC377最小系统”，但并未给出任何关于为何描述与网表型号不同的解释或注释。 --- ### 关键信息缺失 1. **缺失明确的工程变更记录：** 没有找到任何官方说明（如ECN、勘误表、设计注释）来解释为何网表使用 TC367，而接口文档描述为 TC377。 2. **缺失兼容性说明：** 没有证据说明 TC367 和 TC377 在硬件设计上是否完全兼容（如 pin-to-pin兼容），因此无法判断这是否是一个可以忽略的命名错误，还是需要修改设计的重大差异。 --- ### 证据冲突（必须单独列出） 根据提供的证据，存在以下关键冲突，**不应合并为确定结论**： | 冲突字段 | 值1 | 值2 | 来源 | 冲突原因 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | | **器件型号** | **TC367** (`SAK-TC367DP-64F300S AA`) | **TC377** (`SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA`) | 网表（实例 U900A/B） vs 接口文档（模块描述） | 网表实际使用的芯片是 TC367，而接口文档的描述是 TC377。这是最核心的矛盾。 | | 电源电压 | 3.3V | 1.2-1.35V | 接口文档内部（证据[9]） | 同一文档中，“电源需求”部分描述 MCU 需要 VCC3V3_MCU（3.3V±10%）和 VCC1V25_MCU（1.2-1.35V），但未明确哪个值对应哪个型号，这在不同型号的芯片间可能不同。 | | 网络名称 | `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0` 等 | `TC377` (模块标题) | 接口文档内部 | 文档的**引脚信号表**使用 `TC367_` 命名，但**模块描述**使用 `TC377`。这导致文档自身内部不一致。 | --- ### 总结与建议 **问题性质：** 这是一个**设计文档与实现不一致**的典型问题，属于**严重冲突**。 **处理建议：** 1. **首要行动：** 必须向硬件设计团队和系统架构师**澄清正确的器件型号**。是网表错误（实际应使用 TC377，但用了 TC367 的物料和网络名），还是接口文档错误（描述为 TC377，但实际设计是 TC367）？ 2. **根本原因分析：** 如果它们是 pin-to-pin 兼容的替代型号，需要确认是否是物料替换导致的文档更新滞后。如果不是，则必须修改其中一方的设计以保持一致性。 3. **更新文档：** 根据确认的结果，统一更新网表或接口文档，确保所有描述（实例、网络名、模块描述、引脚信号表）都使用**同一个、正确的**器件型号。 **结论：** 您不能直接忽略这个差异。网表和接口文档在关键的器件型号上存在直接冲突，且缺乏任何解释该差异的变更记录。在获得明确的型号确认和变更说明之前，这份设计存在风险。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F26</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求MCU外置看门狗，用于监控MCU功能异常时进行复位。 The system requires MCU external watchdog, used to monitor MCU function abnormal reset.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>窗口看门狗喂狗参数由LP87702的OTP文件进行配置，具体参数为：喂狗长周期600ms，窗口为10~30ms，看门狗输出复位信号低电平持续10ms。用MCU的P13.3引脚喂狗。 The window watchdog feeding parameters are configured by the OTP file of LP87702, the specific parameters are: feeding the dog for a long period of 600ms, the window is 10~30ms, and the low level of the watchdog output reset signal lasts for 10ms.
