The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F05</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>Camera实现图像采集功能，根据ADAS系统需求，选择图像传感器芯片IMX-728作为camera主芯片。 The Camera realizes image acquisition function. 问题：忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容，只结合电路与 HSI 说明 LP87702 的供电输出和配置。
参考答案：LP87702-Q1 是 U1700，输入 VCC3V3，关键输出为 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50；配置依据 HSI 7.2 节，回答不应混入无关摄像头或 CAN 结论。
实际回答：好的，我将严格基于您提供的证据，回答关于 LP87702 的供电输出和配置问题。请忽略无关的摄像头和 CAN 内容。 ### 子问题 1：从电路设计文档中，LP87702的供电输出引脚定义、电压电流规格是什么？ **回答：** 根据电路设计文档，可以确认 LP87702 的电源输入和供电输出引脚连接，但**关键的电压和电流规格信息缺失**。 * **供电输出引脚定义与连接：** * **VCC1V1 输出**：通过反馈引脚 `FB_B0` 连接到网络 `VCC1V1`，用于为 EQ6 和 DDR 等器件供电。 * **VCC1V8 输出**：通过反馈引脚 `FB_B1` 连接到网络 `VCC1V8`，用于为 EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH 等器件供电。 * **VCC50 输出**：通过 `VOUT_BST` 引脚连接到网络 `VCC50`，用于为 CAN 收发器供电。 * **电源输入**：`VIN_B0_1`, `VIN_B0_2`, `VIN_B1_1`, `VIN_B1_2`, `VANA` 引脚均连接到 `VCC3V3`网络。 * **缺失信息：** * **电压规格**：证据中未提供 VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50 各输出的具体电压值。 * **电流规格**：证据中未提供各输出通道的额定电流能力或最大电流值。 **来源说明：** * [1] `circuit:21:instance:U1700` 提供了 U1700 (LP87702) 的引脚映射，显示其 `FB_B0`、`FB_B1`、`VOUT_BST` 等引脚的连接关系。 * [2] `circuit:21:net:VCC1V1` 和 [5] `circuit:21:net:VCC1V8` 说明了这些网络连接的众多器件，印证了其供电范围。 * [9] `circuit:21:instance:U1700` 确认了器件型号为 `LP87702-Q1`。 ### 子问题 2：从HSI文档中，LP87702的配置寄存器、工作模式、上电时序等设置细节是什么？ **回答：** 根据HSI文档，可以确认LP87702的工作模式、使能控制方式以及部分配置细节，但**具体的寄存器地址和数值、以及详细的上电时序图缺失**。 * **工作模式：** LP87702 支持四种运行模式：`SHUTDOWN`、`READ OTP`、`STANDBY`、`ACTIVE`。这些模式切换与电源电压 `VANA` 和复位信号 `NRST` 的电压水平相关。 * **供电输出使能与配置：** * **VCC50 输出**：由 MCU 的 GPIO 引脚控制。MCU 通过引脚 `P15.3`（信号 `MCU_5V_EN`）输出高电平来使能 VCC50。 * **VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 输出**：通过 MCU 的 I2C 接口（引脚 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5`）进行使能和输出电压设置。 * **配置寄存器：** HSI 文档明确提到“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器”，并给出了 **Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration**。然而，**该表格的具体内容（寄存器地址、名称、配置值）在提供的证据中缺失**。 * **上电时序：** HSI 文档中提及了“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器”，但这部分内容被包含在“EQ6L上下电时序要求”的章节中。文档指出 EQ6L 所需的 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 等电源的上下电时序由 MCU 控制，但**具体的时序图（如各电源轨上电顺序、时间间隔要求）未被提供**。 * **功能安全等级：** 功能安全需求为 `QM(B)`。 **来源说明：** * [11] `document:b6943ae371d76077` 描述了 LP87702 的使能方式和供电对象。 * [12] `document:ea7b2234534382bf` 定义了 LP87702 的四种工作模式。 * [13] `P15.3` 用于控制 VCC50 的使能，以及 LP87702 的 I2C 控制功能。 ### 缺失信息总结 1. **LP87702 供电输出的具体电压和电流规格**：电路设计文档中未提供，HSI 文档中虽有 `VCC1V1`、`VCC1V8`、`VCC50` 的软件监控阈值（来自[17]），但这并非器件本身的输出规格。 2. **LP87702 配置寄存器详细内容**：HSI P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable chip, MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set output voltage and communication. +</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN、READ OTP、STANDBY、ACTIVE</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>SHUTDOWN NRST电压低于阈值。LP87702-Q1器件停止工作。 READ OTP 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO电平，并且NRST电压高于阈值电平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平和偏置电路被启用。OTP配置被加载到寄存器。 STANDBY 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。电压转换器被禁用，LP87702-Q1的参考电平、控制和偏压电路被启用。所有的寄存器都可以被主机处理器通过系统串口读写。电压转换器可以根据需要进行使能输出。 ACTIVE 电源电压VANA高于VANAUVLO水平，NRST电压高于阈值水平。稳压DC/DC变换器处于使能状态。所有的寄存器都可以被MCU通过系统串口读写。 SHUTDOWN After the enable signal MCU_OV75_MIPI_DDR_EN=L, the secondary power stops the 0V75_MIPI_DDR power supply. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>1、VANAUVLO阈值电压 2、NRST阈值电压 </td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | ADC电压采集阈值 The ADC voltage acquisition thresholdWCCA计算结果如下，软件应按照如下过欠压阈值设置：The WCCA calculation results are as follows. The timing requirements for the power-on and reset signals of the camera's 3-way power supply are as follows. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | None：U20; None：P23.2; None：P23.2/GTM_TIM1_IN6_5/GTM_TIM0_IN6_5/GTM_TOUT43/CAN12_TXD; None：GPIO; None：MCU_RST_FLASH_P23.2; Reset the chip with P12.1 pin of the MCU. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table>

hw-v1-multihop-eth-enablesuccess0.2300.5840.286not_applicable0.0000.0001.0000.548not_applicable
问题：从 MCU 使能开始，追踪到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。
参考答案：MCU_3V3_ETH_EN 使能 U1500，VCC3V3 经 U1500 形成 VCC3V3_ETH，再进入 U1501 TPS74501，输出 VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据现有证据，无法完整追踪从 MCU 使能到以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源的路径。以下是按子问题组织的分析： --- ### 子问题 1：MCU 的使能信号连接到哪个电源管理芯片或电源轨？ **✅ 已覆盖** - 证据显示，网络 **ECU_EN** 连接到 **U1600** 的 **EN/SYNC** 引脚（证据[12]：`Net ECU_EN connects ... U1600.EN/SYNC`）。 - 该网络来源于 MCU 的 GPIO 输出（未在证据中直接显示，但由 `ECU_EN` 名称推测为 MCU 控制）。 - 此外，MCU 还通过其他 GPIO 输出多个使能信号（如 `CAN_EN_MCU_SPI3_MRST_P02.5`、`MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`、`MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 等），但这些与以太网 PHY 1.0V 电源无关。 **来源**：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF 中的电路设计。 --- ### 子问题 2：该电源管理芯片或电源轨如何产生 1.0V 电压？是否有中间转换器或 LDO？ **⚠️ 缺失** - 证据中未找到任何描述 U1600 或其它芯片如何产生 1.0V 电压的信息。 - 虽然存在网络 `VCC1V0_ETH`（证据[14]：`Net VCC1V0_ETH connects ... U1501.OUT`），但： - 未说明 U1501 的输入来源、输出电压值、是否由 U1600 控制。 - 未有文档或电路图说明 U1501 是 LDO 还是 DC-DC 转换器，也未说明其使能控制。 - 因此，**从 MCU 使能信号（ECU_EN）到 1.0V 电压产生之间的转换机制完全缺失**。 **建议**：需补充查询 U1600 和 U1501 的器件型号、datasheet 或电源树文档。 --- ### 子问题 3：1.0V 电源如何连接到以太网 PHY 的电源引脚？路径上是否有滤波或开关？ **✅ 部分覆盖** - 证据显示网络 `VCC1V0_ETH` 连接到了 **U1501.OUT** 以及多个电容（C1420、C1506、C1507）和电感（L1403）等滤波元件（证据[14]）。 - 但 **未直接证明 `VCC1V0_ETH` 连接到以太网 PHY（U1400）的电源引脚**。U1400 的引脚映射中（证据[1]）只列出了 `VDDA`、`VDDIO`、`VDDMAC`、`VSLEEP`，而未列出它们对应的网络名称。 - 证据中其他网络（如 `PHY_MDIN`、`PHY_MDIP`、`PHY_RESET_N_P22.3`、`PHY_WAKE_P21.5`）均为信号或控制引脚，非电源引脚。 - 因此，**1.0V 电源到 PHY 的最终连接路径未确认**，但存在滤波元件（电容、电感）提示该路径可能经过滤波。 **来源**：部分电路网络（证据[14]）及 PHY 引脚映射（证据[1]）。 --- ### 整体缺口总结 | 缺失环节 | 具体说明 | |----------|----------| | 使能信号如何控制 1.0V 产生 | 从 ECU_EN 到 U1600 的 EN/SYNC 已知，但 U1600 的输出与 1.0V 电压之间的关联未知。 | | 1.0V 电压的产生器件 | 存在 U1501 输出 VCC1V0_ETH，但未确认其输入/输出特性、是否由 U1600 驱动。 | | 1.0V 到 PHY 的最终连接 | VCC1V0_ETH 网络未直接连接到 U1400 的任何电源引脚，PHY 电源引脚的网络名称未知。 | **结论**：现有证据仅能确认 MCU 使能信号（ECU_EN）连接到 U1600，以及存在一个名为 VCC1V0_ETH 的 1.0V 电源网络（带有滤波元件），但无法建立从使能到 PHY 1.0V 电源的完整路径。需要补充如下信息才能完整追踪： - U1600 和 U1501 的器件型号及功能； - 从 U1600 到 U1501 的控制连接； - U1501 的输出电压设定； - PHY（U1400）电源引脚所接的网络名称。
